### 2306.04961 / midloop / alg-001

**检查内容：** The algorithm takes as input a linear operator A, data y, and estimates r̃ and s̃ for the rank and row-sparsity (Algorithm 2.1, line 1).

**类别与权重：** `algorithm_impl`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 0.889%）。

**论文定位：** Algorithm 2.1, line 1。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_04961/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:8)

**如何检查：** algorithm_impl 无单独 verification spec，Judge 对照描述、论文上下文及仓库代码作二元判断。

**A：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:35)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/algorithms.py, the function `irls_lrrs(op: MeasurementOperator, y: np.ndarray, rank: int, sparsity: int, ...)` (line ~55) takes exactly the four inputs described: `op` is the linear operator A (a MeasurementOperator wrapping the matrix `phi`), `y` is the data/measurements, `rank` is the rank estimate r̃, and `sparsity` is the row-sparsity estimate s̃. The docstring explicitly references "Algorithm 2.1" and the exact affine data constraint. All three other baseline algorithms (riemannian\_iht, sparse\_power\_factorization) also accept the same (op, y, rank, sparsity) signature.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The parameter names `rank` and `sparsity` correspond to r̃ and s̃ from the paper's notation. The `MeasurementOperator` wraps the sensing matrix and exposes `.phi`, `.forward()`, and `.adjoint()`, serving as the linear operator A.

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:35)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/core.py, the function `irls_lrrs(op: MeasurementOperator, y: Array, rank: int, sparsity: int, ...)` directly implements Algorithm 2.1. Its parameters correspond exactly to the paper's inputs: `op` is the linear operator A, `y` is the data vector, `rank` is the estimate r̃, and `sparsity` is the estimate s̃ for row-sparsity. The docstring explicitly states "Recover a jointly structured matrix with non-convex IRLS." The module header also notes "The implementation follows Algorithm 2.1 and Definition 2.1 of Kümmerle and Maly (NeurIPS 2023)."

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The parameter names use `rank` and `sparsity` rather than r̃ and s̃, but they serve the exact same role as stated in Algorithm 2.1 line 1.

### 2306.04961 / midloop / alg-002

**检查内容：** The algorithm initializes k=0, sets the initial weight operator W\_{X^{(0)}, ε\_0, δ\_0} = Id, and sets ε\_0 = ∞, δ\_0 = ∞ (Algorithm 2.1, line 2).

**类别与权重：** `algorithm_impl`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 0.889%）。

**论文定位：** Algorithm 2.1, line 2。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_04961/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:17)

**如何检查：** algorithm_impl 无单独 verification spec，Judge 对照描述、论文上下文及仓库代码作二元判断。

**A：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:46)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/algorithms.py, function `irls_lrrs` (starting around line 59): after computing the initial least-squares solution `x` (the k=0 iterate), the code explicitly sets `eps = np.inf` (line ~70) and `delta = np.inf` (line ~71). These correspond to ε\_0 = ∞ and δ\_0 = ∞ from Algorithm 2.1 line 2. The loop counter starts at `iteration = 1`, confirming k=0 is the initialization step. When ε\_0 and δ\_0 are ∞, the weight operator W\_{X^{(0)}, ε\_0, δ\_0} reduces to the identity (all weights become 0/negligible), consistent with W = Id. The first iteration then sets `delta, eps = next_delta, next_eps` via the `if not np.isfinite(delta):` branch.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The identity operator behavior for W at k=0 is implicit (infinite smoothing parameters → identity weighting), not explicitly labeled "Id", but the mathematical equivalence holds. ε\_0 = ∞ and δ\_0 = ∞ are explicitly coded.

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:46)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/core.py, the `irls_lrrs` function initializes: (1) the iteration counter starts at k=0 via `for iteration in range(max_iter)` (with no pre-loop explicit k variable, but the first iteration is 0); (2) `eps = np.inf` (ε\_0 = ∞); (3) `delta = np.inf` (δ\_0 = ∞). These three lines appear just before the main loop (~line 134-135): `eps = np.inf` and `delta = np.inf`. The weight operator W\_{X^{(0)}, ε\_0, δ\_0} = Id is implicit: with ε=∞ and δ=∞, the `_weight_apply` function degenerates to no reweighting (identity), since singular/eps → 0 and row\_w uses delta=∞. This exactly matches Algorithm 2.1, line 2.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> There is no explicit variable named `W` or `k=0` in the code, but the semantic initialization of eps=inf, delta=inf, and iteration starting from 0 fully implements the paper's Algorithm 2.1 line 2. The weight operator being identity at initialization is implicit through the math rather than a named variable.

### 2306.04961 / midloop / alg-003

**检查内容：** For each iteration k=1,...,K, the new iterate X^{(k)} is computed by solving the weighted least squares problem: X^{(k)} = argmin\_{Z: A(Z)=y} ∥W\_{X^{(k-1)}, ε\_{k-1}, δ\_{k-1}}^{-1/2} Z∥\_F^2 (Algorithm 2.1, line 4).

**类别与权重：** `algorithm_impl`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 0.889%）。

**论文定位：** Algorithm 2.1, line 4。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_04961/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:26)

**如何检查：** algorithm_impl 无单独 verification spec，Judge 对照描述、论文上下文及仓库代码作二元判断。

**A：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:57)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/algorithms.py, the irls\_lrrs function computes weights (row\_w from row norms, sv\_w from singular values) consistent with W\_{X^{(k-1)}, ε\_{k-1}, δ\_{k-1}}, but does NOT solve the constrained weighted least-squares problem argmin\_{Z: A(Z)=y} ∥W^{-1/2} Z∥\_F^2. Instead, it uses a projected gradient / preconditioned gradient descent: it computes grad = row\_w\[:,None\]\*x + spectral\_grad (lines ~100-102), takes a gradient step x - step\*grad, applies hard-thresholding (\_rank\_threshold, \_row\_threshold), then projects onto A(Z)=y via \_projection(). The code comment explicitly says "This avoids forming the ambient n1\*n2 normal matrix" — confirming it is a gradient-based approximation, not a direct WLS solve.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The implementation uses the same weights as prescribed but replaces the exact constrained WLS solve with a preconditioned projected-gradient iteration with hard-thresholding projections — a different computational approach that does not exactly solve argmin\_{A(Z)=y} ∥W^{-1/2} Z∥\_F^2.

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:57)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/core.py, the function `irls_lrrs` implements Algorithm 2.1. At each outer iteration, it: (1) computes SVD/row-norm weights from the current iterate x (lines ~147-152), (2) runs `inner_steps` projected preconditioned gradient steps (lines ~155-163) that minimize `‖W^{-1/2} Z‖_F^2` subject to `A(Z)=y`. The `_weight_apply` function (line ~85-96) applies Definition 2.1's combined low-rank plus row-sparsity weight operator W\_{X^{(k-1)},ε,δ}. Each inner step computes `weighted = _weight_apply(x, ua, va, sa, delta, p_sparse)` (the gradient of ‖W^{-1/2}Z‖\_F^2) and then projects back onto the feasible set via `_project_measurements`. The comment at line ~154 explicitly says: 'A few projected steps solve the changing weighted least-squares model.' This directly implements the WLS subproblem in Algorithm 2.1 line 4 via an approximate iterative solver.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The subproblem is solved approximately (via 8 projected gradient steps) rather than in closed form, but this is the standard IRLS implementation approach and correctly implements the WLS structure from Algorithm 2.1 line 4.

### 2306.04961 / midloop / alg-004

**检查内容：** After computing X^{(k)}, the algorithm computes the (r̃+1)-st singular value σ\_{r̃+1}(X^{(k)}) and the (s̃+1)-st largest row ℓ2-norm ρ\_{s̃+1}(X^{(k)}) (Algorithm 2.1, line 5).

**类别与权重：** `algorithm_impl`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 0.889%）。

**论文定位：** Algorithm 2.1, line 5。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_04961/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:35)

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:68)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/algorithms.py, within the `irls_lrrs` function (Algorithm 2.1), at the start of each iteration: `rows = np.linalg.norm(x, axis=1)` computes all row ℓ2-norms; `singular = np.linalg.svd(x, compute_uv=False)` computes all singular values. Then `next_eps = max(float(singular[min(rank, len(singular) - 1)]), 1e-8)` extracts the (rank+1)-st singular value σ\_{r̃+1}(X^(k)), and `next_delta = max(float(np.sort(rows)[::-1][min(sparsity, len(rows) - 1)]), 1e-8)` extracts the (sparsity+1)-st largest row ℓ2-norm ρ\_{s̃+1}(X^(k)). These correspond exactly to Algorithm 2.1, line 5.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The variable names differ from the paper (rank→r̃, sparsity→s̃, next\_eps→σ\_{r̃+1}, next\_delta→ρ\_{s̃+1}) but the mathematical operations are identical.

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:68)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/core.py, function irls\_lrrs (around lines 140-160): after updating x (X^{(k)}), the code computes `singular = np.linalg.svd(x, compute_uv=False)` and then `eps_target = singular[rank]` which extracts the (r̃+1)-st singular value σ\_{r̃+1}(X^{(k)}) (0-indexed: index `rank` = (rank+1)-th value). Similarly, `rows = _row_norms(x)` computes row ℓ2-norms, and `delta_target = np.sort(rows)[::-1][sparsity]` extracts the (s̃+1)-st largest row ℓ2-norm ρ\_{s̃+1}(X^{(k)}) (0-indexed: index `sparsity` after descending sort).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The variable names differ from the paper (eps\_target/delta\_target vs σ\_{r̃+1}/ρ\_{s̃+1}), but the mathematical operations match exactly what Algorithm 2.1, line 5 requires.

### 2306.04961 / midloop / alg-007

**检查内容：** The weight operator W\_{X^{(k)}, ε\_k, δ\_k} is defined as W\_{X^{(k)}, ε\_k, δ\_k}(Z) = W^{lr}\_{X^{(k)}, ε\_k}(Z) + Z · W^{sp}\_{X^{(k)}, δ\_k}, where W^{lr} is a low-rank promoting operator and W^{sp} is a diagonal matrix (Definition 2.1).

**类别与权重：** `algorithm_impl`；`secondary`；权重 1（本层占比 0.444%）。

**论文定位：** Definition 2.1。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_04961/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:62)

**如何检查：** algorithm_impl 无单独 verification spec，Judge 对照描述、论文上下文及仓库代码作二元判断。

**A：通过，1/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:101)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/algorithms.py, the irls\_lrrs function (lines ~90-105) computes the weight operator as a sum of two components. The comment explicitly says "\# W = W\_sp + W\_lr". The sparse part is `row_w[:, None] * x` where `row_w = 1/max(rows, delta)^2` (a diagonal matrix applied to Z row-by-row, i.e., W^{sp} times Z). The low-rank part is `spectral_grad = (u * (sv_w * s)[None, :]) @ vt` where `sv_w = 1/max(s, eps)^2` — this is the SVD-based low-rank promoting operator W^{lr}(Z). The total gradient is `grad = row_w[:, None] * x + spectral_grad`, exactly matching Definition 2.1: W(Z) = W^{lr}(Z) + Z · W^{sp}.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The sparse weight W^{sp} is implemented as element-wise row scaling (diagonal matrix with row-norm-based weights), and W^{lr} is implemented via the SVD-based spectral gradient. The sum structure matches exactly.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/core.py, the function `_weight_apply` (around line 87) is explicitly documented as "Apply Definition 2.1's low-rank plus row-sparsity weight operator." It computes: (1) W^lr part: `low = left @ x @ right` where left and right are identity-plus-rank-correction matrices that implement the low-rank promoting operator W^lr\_{X^{(k)}, ε\_k}(Z); (2) W^sp part: `row_w[:, None] * x` where `row_w = rows**(p-2.0)` are per-row scalars derived from row norms — this is a diagonal matrix multiplication W^sp\_{X^{(k)}, δ\_k} applied to Z; (3) Final return: `low + row_w[:, None] * x` = W^lr(Z) + Z · W^sp, matching the formula in Definition 2.1 exactly. The function is called in `irls_lrrs` as `weighted = _weight_apply(x, ua, va, sa, delta, p_sparse)` in each inner iteration.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The diagonal W^sp is implemented as per-row scalar weights (row\_w\[:, None\] \* x), which is mathematically equivalent to right-multiplying by a diagonal matrix when the structure is row-wise. The low-rank operator W^lr is implemented via left/right matrix products without explicitly constructing the perpendicular complement.

### 2306.04961 / midloop / alg-008

**检查内容：** The low-rank weight operator W^{lr}\_{X^{(k)}, ε\_k} is defined using the singular value decomposition of X^{(k)}; the matrices U⊥ and V⊥, which are arbitrary complementary orthogonal bases for the left and right singular vector matrices U and V, do not need to be computed in the algorithm (Definition 2.1).

**论文定位：** Definition 2.1。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_04961/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:71)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/algorithms.py, the low-rank weight operator is implemented in the `irls_lrrs` function (lines 100–106). At line 103, `np.linalg.svd(x, full_matrices=False)` is called with `full_matrices=False`, computing only the thin SVD (U, S, Vt) without the complementary bases U⊥ or V⊥. At line 105, the spectral gradient — which corresponds to W^{lr} applied to X — is computed as `(u * (sv_w * s)[None, :]) @ vt`, purely using the singular values and singular vectors from the thin SVD. The same pattern appears at line 122 in the refinement loop. U⊥ and V⊥ are never computed anywhere in the algorithm. A comment at line 100 explicitly notes "W = W\_sp + W\_lr".

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The implementation computes the action of W^{lr} on X directly (spectral gradient form) rather than explicitly constructing the operator matrix, but this is mathematically equivalent and consistent with the claim that U⊥/V⊥ need not be computed.

**B：通过，1/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:112)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/core.py, the function `_weight_apply` (lines ~87-98) implements Definition 2.1's low-rank weight operator W^{lr}. It computes the operator as: left = I + U @ diag(1/sigma - 1) @ U^H and right = I + V @ diag(1/sigma - 1) @ V^H, then applies low = left @ x @ right. This identity-plus-correction formulation mathematically avoids computing U⊥ and V⊥ explicitly (the comment says "without constructing U\_perp/V\_perp"). In `irls_lrrs`, the SVD of X^(k) is computed via `np.linalg.svd(x, full_matrices=False)` (full\_matrices=False means only U and V are returned, not the full orthogonal completions U⊥, V⊥), and only the active singular vectors `ua, va, sa` are passed to `_weight_apply`.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The implementation uses the algebraic identity I + U(diag(1/σ)-I)U^H = U·diag(1/σ)·U^H + U⊥·I·U⊥^H without explicitly forming U⊥, which is exactly what Definition 2.1 says (U⊥ need not be computed).

### 2306.04961 / midloop / alg-009

**检查内容：** The sparsity weight matrix W^{sp}\_{X^{(k)}, δ\_k} is a diagonal matrix whose entries are computed from the ℓ2-norms of the rows of X^{(k)} and the smoothing parameter δ\_k (Definition 2.1).

**类别与权重：** `algorithm_impl`；`secondary`；权重 1（本层占比 0.444%）。

**论文定位：** Definition 2.1。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_04961/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:80)

**A：通过，1/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:123)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/algorithms.py, function irls\_lrrs (lines ~100-105): `rows = np.linalg.norm(x, axis=1)` computes the ℓ2-norms of the rows of X^(k), then `row_w = np.minimum(1.0 / np.maximum(rows, delta) ** 2, 1e6)` computes the diagonal entries of W^{sp} as 1/max(‖x\_i‖, δ\_k)^2, matching Definition 2.1. The diagonal matrix is applied as `row_w[:, None] * x`, which is equivalent to diag(row\_w) @ x. The variable `delta` is the smoothing parameter δ\_k updated each iteration.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The implementation caps entries at 1e6 for numerical stability (equivalent to the smoothed version where the denominator never goes below sqrt(1/1e6)); this is a standard regularization in IRLS that is consistent with Definition 2.1's use of the smoothing parameter δ\_k.

**B：通过，1/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:123)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/core.py, the function `_row_norms(x)` (line ~74) computes ℓ2-norms of all rows: `np.sqrt(np.sum(np.abs(x)**2, axis=1))`. The function `_weight_apply` (line ~77) then computes: `rows = np.maximum(_row_norms(x), delta)` (incorporating smoothing parameter δ) and `row_w = rows ** (p - 2.0)` (with p=0 default giving `rows^(-2)`). The application `row_w[:, None] * x` implements left-multiplication by a diagonal matrix whose i-th diagonal entry is `max(||x_i||_2, δ)^(p-2)`, exactly matching Definition 2.1's W^{sp}\_{X^(k), δ\_k}. The matrix is not explicitly constructed as a dense diagonal array but is applied as row-wise scaling, which is mathematically identical to a diagonal matrix.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The implementation uses implicit diagonal matrix application (row-wise scaling) rather than an explicit `np.diag(...)` construction, but this is a standard and equivalent representation. The parameter `p` defaults to 0.0 in `irls_lrrs` call, matching the p=0 case of the paper's Definition 2.1.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

The weights `row_w = 1/max(rows, delta)^2` and `sv_w = 1/max(s, eps)^2` are then applied uniformly to all rows/singular values. The code never explicitly computes r\_k = \|{i: σ\_i(X^(k)) \> ε\_k}\| or s\_k = \|{i: ‖X^(k)\_{i,:}‖ \> δ\_k}\| as dynamic counts, nor uses these counts within the weight operator construction per Definition 2.1. The counts are implicitly assumed to equal the fixed `rank` and `sparsity` parameters, not tracked as dynamically-updated quantities r\_k and s\_k that appear in the weight operator definition.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

**B：未通过，0/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:134)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

However, s\_k = \|{i: ‖X^(k)\_{i,:}‖\_2 \> δ\_k}\| is never computed anywhere — row weighting in `_weight_apply` uses a continuous formula `rows = np.maximum(_row_norms(x), delta); row_w = rows ** (p - 2.0)` that does not depend on the count of rows exceeding δ\_k. The `_weight_apply` function signature and body contain no s\_k parameter or calculation.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> r\_k is present implicitly as `active`, but s\_k (the count of row norms exceeding delta) is completely absent from the weight operator and from the broader algorithm, so the full claim (both r\_k and s\_k used in Definition 2.1's weight operator) is not satisfied.

Since "estimated\_rank" and "estimated\_sparsity" are only overridden in the "misspecification" experiment, all other experiments (including "phase\_transition\_dense\_rank1", the rank-1 Gaussian experiment) use erank=rank=1 and esparsity=sparsity=40. All three algorithms — `irls_lrrs`, `sparse_power_factorization`, and `riemannian_iht` — are called with the same `erank` and `esparsity` parameters. Thus all methods receive oracle knowledge of the true rank r=1 and true row-sparsity s=40.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

In run\_phase\_transition (line 78), rank=1 is hardcoded and sparsity values from the ground truth configuration are passed through — no estimation occurs. All methods receive oracle (true) rank r=1 and true sparsity s.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The phase transition experiment uses n1=256, n2=40, rank=1, sparsity=\[40\], consistent with oracle knowledge of both r and s.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/algorithms.py, the `irls_lrrs` function (line 55) has default parameters `max_iter: int = 250` (line 63) and `tol: float = 1e-10` (line 64). The stopping check at line 138 reads: `if previous is not None and np.linalg.norm(x - previous) / max(np.linalg.norm(x), 1e-15) < tol: break`. This computes the relative Frobenius norm change ‖X^(k)−X^(k−1)‖\_F / ‖X^(k)‖\_F and breaks when it is less than 1e-10, or after at most 250 iterations (line 91: `for iteration in range(1, max_iter + 1)`).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The denominator uses `max(np.linalg.norm(x), 1e-15)` to avoid division by zero, which is a minor numerical guard but equivalent to the paper's criterion in practice.

The formula for relative Frobenius norm change is correctly implemented (line 211), but the paper-specified values (tol=1e-10, max\_iter=250) are not used anywhere for IRLS. The value 250 appears only as the default max\_iter for projected\_gradient() (line 294), not for IRLS.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The stopping criterion formula matches (relative Frobenius norm), but both the tolerance (1e-8 vs 1e-10) and max iterations (50 default / 3-8 in practice vs 250) differ from the claim.

The stopping criterion at line 280 is `if rel < 1e-8: break`, where rel is the relative change of the combined product matrix `current = u @ v.T` divided by its norm (line 277-278). This does not match the rubric's claim of (1) tol=1e-10, (2) separate checks ∥U\_k−U\_{k−1}∥ \< tol and ∥V\_k−V\_{k−1}∥ \< tol. The max\_iter=250 default (line 294) is correct.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The code uses tol=1e-8 (not 1e-10) and checks the relative change of the product matrix U@V^T rather than separate absolute norms of the U and V factor matrices.

However, the stopping criterion at line 195-196 is: `change = np.linalg.norm(x - previous) / max(np.linalg.norm(x), 1e-15); if change < tol: break` — this checks relative change in the iterate x, NOT the norm of the Riemannian gradient. The stopping criterion is `rel < 1e-8` (line 332), where `rel` is the relative Frobenius-norm change in the iterate x, not the norm of the Riemannian gradient. The default max\_iter is 250 (line 294), and for the phase transition experiment (run\_experiments.py line 89) it is called with `max_iter=8`. Neither the tolerance value (1e-10 vs 1e-8), the convergence measure (Riemannian gradient norm vs relative iterate change), nor the max iterations (2000 vs 8/250) match the rubric.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The implementation uses a completely different stopping criterion (relative change in iterate, not gradient norm) and far fewer max iterations than specified in the rubric.

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:266)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/algorithms.py, the `irls_lrrs` function initializes `x` via `_initial_ls(op, y)` (around line 70), which computes `gram_inv = np.linalg.pinv(phi @ phi.conj().T, rcond=1e-12)` and `x = (phi.conj().T @ (gram_inv @ y)).reshape(...)` — the exact Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse / unweighted least-squares solution. The IRLS weight parameters `eps` and `delta` are initialized to `np.inf`, and the weighted updates (`row_w`, `sv_w`) are only computed inside the loop starting at iteration 1, using the current `x`. Thus the first "iteration" effectively solves an unweighted (identity-weight) least squares problem, consistent with the claim.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The initialization is done outside the main loop, and the first loop iteration then computes weights from the unweighted solution. This matches "identity weight operator in first iteration" semantics.

**B：未通过，0/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:266)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/core.py, the `irls_lrrs` function initializes `eps = np.inf` and `delta = np.inf` before the loop, but at the very start of the first iteration (lines inside `for iteration in range(max_iter):`), it immediately updates `eps` and `delta` to finite values derived from the current `x` (e.g., `eps_target = singular[rank]`, `delta_target = np.sort(rows)[::-1][sparsity]`). The weight operator applied in `_weight_apply` uses these finite `eps`/`delta` values, so the first inner solve is **not** unweighted. For the weight to be identity, `eps` would need to remain `np.inf` during the first iteration's inner steps, but the code resets it to a finite data-dependent value before any inner step runs.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The paper (Algorithm 2.1) typically starts with eps=inf/delta=inf so the first iteration reduces to an unweighted (identity-weighted) least-squares problem, but this implementation updates the smoothing parameters before applying them in each iteration, including the first.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

The phase-transition experiment (run\_experiments.py line 97) and convergence experiment both use n1=256, n2=40, rank=5, matching the rubric's R^{256×5}, R^{5}, R^{40×5} dimensions.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

**A：通过，1/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:387)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/algorithms.py, the IRLS function `irls_lrrs` initializes `x` via `_initial_ls(op, y)` (lines ~57-67), which computes the minimum-norm unweighted least squares solution using the pseudoinverse of the Gram matrix. The smoothing parameters `eps` and `delta` are initialized to `np.inf`. In the first loop iteration, the branch `if not np.isfinite(delta):` is true, setting delta/eps based on the current (unweighted LS) x. This implements "weight operator = identity" for the initialization step, solving an unweighted LS in the first iteration as described in Algorithm 2.1.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The unweighted LS solve happens before the iterative loop (not inside it as "iteration 1"), but this is functionally equivalent to what Algorithm 2.1 describes as initializing with the identity weight operator.

**B：通过，1/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:387)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/core.py, irls\_lrrs initializes eps=np.inf and delta=np.inf (lines 173-174). In the first iteration, the SVD-based active rank is 0 (since no singular values exceed np.inf), so ua/va/sa are empty. In \_weight\_apply (lines 117-130), when u.size==0 the low-rank part returns x unchanged, and row\_w = np.maximum(rows, np.inf)**(p-2) = np.inf**(-2) = 0, so the row term is also zero. The total weighted output equals x, meaning the weight operator acts as the identity. The inner loop then minimizes an unweighted quadratic (standard least squares), exactly matching Algorithm 2.1's initialization.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The weight is implicitly the identity via eps=inf and delta=inf degenerating both low-rank and row-sparsity reweighting terms to zero/identity in iteration 1.

All entries drawn i.i.d. standard Gaussian, U\* has exactly s=sparsity non-zero rows.

The stopping criterion at line 138 is: `np.linalg.norm(x - previous) / max(np.linalg.norm(x), 1e-15) < tol`, which is the relative change of the Frobenius norm (np.linalg.norm of a 2D matrix defaults to Frobenius norm) less than 1e-10, checked after each of the up to 250 iterations.

In run\_experiments.py line 67, the IRLS call uses tol=1e-8 explicitly and max\_iter values of 35, 8, or 3 depending on experiment — never 250. The claimed threshold of 1e-10 and maximum of 250 iterations do not appear anywhere in the codebase.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The relative change of Frobenius norm stopping criterion is implemented, but with tol=1e-8 (not 1e-10) and max\_iter well below 250. The 250 default appears only in \_spf\_reference (line 294), not in IRLS.

However, the stopping criterion at line 247-248 is: `change = np.linalg.norm(x - previous) / max(np.linalg.norm(x), 1e-15); if change < tol: break` where x = u @ v.T. This checks a relative norm of the change in the full matrix product x, NOT separate absolute norms on U and V factors. The rubric requires \|\|U\_k - U\_{k-1}\|\| \< 1e-10 AND \|\|V\_k - V\_{k-1}\|\| \< 1e-10 (two separate checks on individual factors with absolute norms).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The max\_iter=250 and tol=1e-10 values match, but the stopping criterion structure differs: code uses one relative-norm check on the matrix product x rather than two absolute-norm checks on factors U and V individually.

**B：未通过，0/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:508)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/core.py, the SPF implementation is in `_spf_reference` (line 254). The maximum iterations default is 250 (set in `projected_gradient` at line 294). However, the stopping criterion does NOT match the rubric: the code checks `rel = np.linalg.norm(current - previous, "fro") / max(np.linalg.norm(current, "fro"), 1e-14)` and breaks at `rel < 1e-8` (line ~282). The rubric specifies (1) separate checks on factor matrices U and V with `||U_k - U_{k-1}|| < 1e-10` and `||V_k - V_{k-1}|| < 1e-10`, but the code checks the combined matrix product `U@V.T` as a single relative (not absolute) norm, and (2) the threshold is `1e-8`, not `1e-10`.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> max\_iter=250 is correct, but both the form (single combined matrix norm vs separate U and V norms) and threshold value (1e-8 vs 1e-10) differ from the rubric. The code also uses a relative norm (divided by \|\|current\|\|), while the rubric implies an absolute norm comparison.

### 2306.04961 / midloop / setup-036

**检查内容：** The weight operator of IRLS is initialized by the identity, solving an unweighted least squares problem in the first iteration.

**类别与权重：** `exp_setup`；`secondary`；权重 1（本层占比 0.444%）。

**论文定位：** Section 4, Algorithm 2.1。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_04961/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:710)

**如何检查：** 目标实验=exp3\_phase\_transition\_r5\_overestimated\_gaussian；数据集=synthetic Gaussian；方法=IRLS；按scope/描述检查是否满足；满足得全部权重，否则得0。

**完整验证规则：**

```json
{
  "type": "boolean",
  "spec": {
    "experiment_id": "exp3_phase_transition_r5_overestimated_gaussian",
    "method": "IRLS",
    "dataset": "synthetic Gaussian",
    "variant": ""
  }
}
```

**A：通过，1/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:541)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In `simirls/algorithms.py`, the `irls_lrrs` function (line 80) calls `_initial_ls(op, y)` before the IRLS loop. `_initial_ls` (lines 47-54) computes the Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse solution via `(phi.conj().T @ (gram_inv @ y))`, which is the unweighted least-squares solution — equivalent to W=I (identity weight operator). The smoothing parameters are also initialized to `eps = np.inf` and `delta = np.inf` (lines 82-83). This pattern directly implements "initialized by the identity, solving an unweighted least squares problem in the first iteration" as described in Algorithm 2.1.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The "first iteration" in the paper corresponds to the initialization step before the main loop in the code. The loop body itself (iterations 1..max\_iter) uses data-adaptive weights computed from the current iterate, but the zeroth step that seeds the loop is the unweighted LS solution.

**B：未通过，0/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:541)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In `simirls/core.py`, `irls_lrrs` initializes `eps = np.inf` and `delta = np.inf`, but at the TOP of iteration 0 (before the inner weighted loop), it immediately updates them: `eps = max(eps_target, 1e-12)` (since `np.isfinite(np.inf)` is False) and similarly for `delta`. Thus the weight operator used in the first iteration's inner loop reflects the SVD/row-norm of the initial x, NOT the identity. For the identity weight, one would need `ua = []` (no active singular values) or `sa` all ones and `row_w` all ones. Instead, `active = min(rank, sum(s > eps+1e-12))` and `sa = s[:active]/eps` give non-trivial weights from the first iteration. The claim that the first iteration solves an unweighted (identity-weighted) least squares problem is not satisfied.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> Algorithm 2.1 in many IRLS papers does specify starting with identity weights; this implementation skips that and uses data-driven eps/delta from iteration 1 onward.

No r\_tilde=10 or s\_tilde=floor(1.5\*s) parameters exist anywhere in the codebase. The experiment 'exp3\_phase\_transition\_r5\_overestimated\_gaussian' with variant 'overestimated' has no corresponding implementation in run\_experiments.py either.

The `relative_error` / Relative Frobenius error column is only used in `noise()` (line 47) and `operators()` (line 58), which correspond to other figures. ### 2306.04961 / midloop / setup-048

**检查内容：** In the convergence rate experiment, the IRLS weight operator is initialized by the identity, so the first iteration solves an unweighted least squares problem.

**类别与权重：** `exp_setup`；`secondary`；权重 1（本层占比 0.444%）。

**论文定位：** Section 4, Numerical Evaluation。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_04961/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:917)

**如何检查：** 目标实验=exp4\_convergence\_behavior；数据集=synthetic Gaussian n1=256 n2=40 r=5 s=40 m=1125；方法=IRLS；按scope/描述检查是否满足；满足得全部权重，否则得0。

**完整验证规则：**

```json
{
  "type": "boolean",
  "spec": {
    "experiment_id": "exp4_convergence_behavior",
    "method": "IRLS",
    "dataset": "synthetic Gaussian n1=256 n2=40 r=5 s=40 m=1125"
  }
}
```

**A：通过，1/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:673)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/algorithms.py, the irls\_lrrs function (line 80) calls \_initial\_ls(op, y) before the iteration loop. \_initial\_ls (lines 47-54) solves the unweighted least-squares problem x = Φ⁺ y (Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse), equivalent to using identity weights. Additionally, eps = np.inf and delta = np.inf (lines 82-83) initialize the smoothing parameters to infinity, which is the IRLS convention for "identity weights" at the start. This means the first iterate is obtained by plain (unweighted) least squares, as stated in the claim.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The unweighted LS solve happens in \_initial\_ls before the for-loop. The first for-loop iteration then updates delta/eps from the current x (via the `not np.isfinite(delta)` branch) and applies computed weights. So "first iteration = unweighted LS" refers to the \_initial\_ls initialization step (iteration 0 in IRLS parlance), not the first pass of the explicit for-loop.

**B：未通过，0/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:673)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/core.py, irls\_lrrs() initializes eps=np.inf and delta=np.inf (lines 173-174). At the start of the first iteration (line 185-186), eps and delta are immediately updated from np.inf to finite targets (eps\_target from the SVD of the initial x, delta\_target from row norms). The \_weight\_apply() function then uses these finite eps/delta values to build a non-trivial weight operator (lines 122-130). There is no special "identity" initialization that would make the first iteration solve an unweighted least squares problem. The first inner solve uses weights derived from the SVD of the initial x (which is the least-squares solution of zeros), not identity weights.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The paper may describe initializing the weight operator as identity (eps=inf, delta=inf) causing the first iteration to be unweighted least squares, but the code immediately overwrites eps/delta before applying weights, so the first iteration is NOT an unweighted least squares solve.

The formula matches the claim. However, the default tol is 1e-8 (line 154), not 1e-10. In run\_experiments.py, run\_convergence() calls run\_case(..., max\_iter=8, inner\_steps=2) with no tol argument (line ~101), and the general run\_case call (line 67) also explicitly passes tol=1e-8. No call in the convergence experiment uses tol=1e-10.

This checks a single relative Frobenius-norm change in the full matrix product (not separate factor norms), and uses threshold 1e-8 (not 1e-10). The claim requires two separate checks \|\|U\_k - U\_{k-1}\|\| \< 1e-10 and \|\|V\_k - V\_{k-1}\|\| \< 1e-10, which are not present anywhere in the codebase.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

This matches claim about iid standard Gaussian a\_j and b\_j for j=1,...,m.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

Each row j of `a` and `b` gives independent standard Gaussian vectors a\_j ∈ R^n1 and b\_j ∈ R^n2, and the outer product a\_j ⊗ b\_j vectorized defines the j-th measurement, matching (A.1) of the paper.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The scaling factor 1/sqrt(m) is applied but this is a normalization convention and does not change the Gaussian nature of the vectors.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In run\_experiments.py line 71-72, success is computed as: `err = relative_error(rec, truth)` followed by `"success": bool(err < 1e-4)`. The `relative_error` function in simirls/core.py lines 337-338 is: `return float(np.linalg.norm(x - truth, "fro") / max(np.linalg.norm(truth, "fro"), 1e-14))` — exactly the relative Frobenius error \|\|X-X\*\|\|\_F/\|\|X\*\|\|\_F. The threshold 1e-4 = 10^-4 matches the claim precisely.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The function uses a small epsilon (1e-14) in the denominator to avoid division by zero, which is numerically equivalent to the paper's definition for any nonzero X\*.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

For the `operator_comparison` experiment (which matches `random_rank1_measurements_r1`/`exp5`), line 141 calls `run_case(... max_iter=3, ...)`. For SPF specifically (line 69), the actual max\_iter passed is `max_iter * 2`, so SPF gets `3 * 2 = 6` iterations in this experiment — not 250. The value 250 appears only as the default parameter in `simirls/core.py` line 294 (`max_iter: int = 250`), but this default is never reached for this experiment since the caller explicitly overrides it with 6.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The `projected_gradient` function has a default of `max_iter=250`, but the experiment-specific call path for `operator_comparison` (the experiment corresponding to random rank-1 measurements) sets `max_iter=3` for `run_case`, which translates to SPF getting `3*2=6` iterations, not 250.

However, RiemAdaIHT (projected\_gradient with variant='riem', ~line 305) initializes from zero: `x = _project_measurements(np.zeros((op.n1, op.n2)), op, y, gram_inv)` — a projection of the zero matrix onto the measurement set, not spectral initialization. The claim that BOTH methods use spectral initialization is therefore false.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> SPF uses spectral init; RiemAdaIHT uses zero-initialization followed by measurement projection. Only half the claim holds.

### 2306.04961 / midloop / setup-071

**检查内容：** IRLS initializes its weight operator by the identity, solving an unweighted least squares problem in the first iteration.

**类别与权重：** `exp_setup`；`secondary`；权重 1（本层占比 0.444%）。

**论文定位：** Section 4。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_04961/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:1308)

**如何检查：** 目标实验=exp5\_random\_rank1\_measurements\_r1；数据集=random\_rank1\_measurements\_r1；方法=IRLS；按scope/描述检查是否满足；满足得全部权重，否则得0。

**A：通过，1/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:926)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/algorithms.py, irls\_lrrs() begins by calling \_initial\_ls(op, y), which computes x = (phi.conj().T @ (gram\_inv @ y)).reshape(...) — the minimum-norm unweighted least squares solution (lines ~49-54, ~66-67). This is the identity-weight initialization: W₀=I gives an ordinary LS problem. The variables eps=np.inf and delta=np.inf are set before the loop, and the first loop iteration updates them from this initial solution before computing any weighted gradient step. This is the standard IRLS pattern where the 0th iterate solves an unweighted LS (identity weight operator).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The unweighted LS is solved as a pre-loop initialization (not labeled "iteration 1" in the code), but this is the standard IRLS convention — W₀=I produces the initial x, subsequent iterations use reweighted steps.

**B：未通过，0/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:926)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/core.py, irls\_lrrs() initializes x = \_project\_measurements(np.zeros(...), ...) (minimum-norm feasible point), eps=inf, delta=inf. On the first iteration (lines 179-196): eps and delta are updated to finite values from the SVD/row-norms of x; active=0 for near-zero x, so \_weight\_apply (lines 117-130) returns `x + row_w*x = (1 + delta^(p-2))*x` — not the identity operator. There is no explicit identity initialization of the weight operator, and the first inner solve is not an unweighted least-squares problem (the row-sparsity weight term is active from the very first inner step).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The paper claims IRLS initializes W=I (identity) so the first iteration solves plain least squares. The code instead initializes x to the minimum-norm feasible point directly, and even on the first inner iteration applies a non-identity weight that includes the row-sparsity term scaled by delta^(p-2).

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:937)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> simirls/data.py, function generate\_ground\_truth (lines 34-66): (1) s non-zero rows chosen uniformly at random via rng.choice(n1, size=sparsity, replace=False) (line 53); (2) U⋆ (left) filled with standard\_normal entries at those rows (lines 54-55); (3) d⋆ (scales) drawn as rng.standard\_normal(rank) when condition\_number is None (line 58); (4) V⋆ (right) drawn as rng.standard\_normal((n2, rank)) (line 56); (5) X̃⋆ = (left \* scales) @ right.T (line 62); (6) normalized by Frobenius norm: x / norm where norm = np.linalg.norm(x, "fro") (lines 63, 66).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The implementation uses diag(d⋆) embedded as left \* scales\[None, :\] before multiplying by right.T, which is algebraically identical to U⋆ diag(d⋆) V⋆\*. The condition\_number branch (geometric scales) is not used in the standard experiment setup.

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:937)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> simirls/core.py lines 53–84: `generate_ground_truth` constructs X⋆ exactly as claimed. (1) Support of s rows chosen uniformly at random: `support = np.sort(rng.choice(n1, size=sparsity, replace=False))`. (2) U⋆ with s non-zero rows of i.i.d. standard Gaussian entries: `u = np.zeros((n1, rank)); u[support] = rng.standard_normal((sparsity, rank))`. (3) d⋆ i.i.d. standard Gaussian: `d = rng.standard_normal(rank)`. (4) V⋆ i.i.d. standard Gaussian: `v = rng.standard_normal((n2, rank))`. (5) X̃⋆ = U⋆ diag(d⋆) V⋆\*: `x = (u * d[None, :]) @ v.conj().T`. (6) Normalization to Frobenius norm 1: `x = x / max(np.linalg.norm(x, "fro"), ...)`. This matches the paper's Appendix A.1 model exactly.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The implementation also supports complex-valued matrices via a flag, but the default real-valued path matches the rubric exactly.

**A：通过，1/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:1036)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/algorithms.py, the `irls_lrrs` function (line 80) calls `_initial_ls(op, y)` before the main loop. `_initial_ls` (lines 47–54) computes `gram_inv = np.linalg.pinv(phi @ phi.conj().T)` and `x = (phi.conj().T @ gram_inv @ y).reshape(...)`, which is the minimum-norm (unweighted, W=I) least-squares solution. This is the standard IRLS initialization with identity weights, giving an unweighted LS problem as the first (zeroth) step before the weighted iterations in the loop begin.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The unweighted LS solve is done as a pre-loop initialization step rather than as a labeled "iteration 1" inside the loop, but this is precisely the standard IRLS pattern where W₀=I. The loop thereafter uses data-driven weights from row norms and singular values.

**B：未通过，0/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:1036)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/core.py, the irls\_lrrs function sets eps=np.inf and delta=np.inf before the loop, but at the very start of iteration 0 it immediately updates: eps = max(min(eps, eps\_target) if np.isfinite(eps) else eps\_target, 1e-12) — since np.inf is not finite, eps becomes eps\_target (the (rank+1)-th singular value), and similarly delta becomes a finite value. Then \_weight\_apply is called with ua, va, sa where sa = s\[:active\]/max(eps,1e-12), and row\_w = rows\*\*(p-2). With p\_sparse=0, row\_w = rows\*\*(-2) — a non-trivial (non-identity) weighting. There is no iteration where the weight operator is the identity; the first iteration already uses non-trivial singular-value and row weights.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The claim would require eps=inf and delta=inf to persist into the first call to \_weight\_apply, making all weights equal to 1 (identity). Instead, eps and delta are updated to finite values before the first weight application.

The conjugation is applied to FB rather than FA compared to the paper formula, but since A and B are i.i.d. real Gaussian this is statistically equivalent.

**B：通过，2/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:1069)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> simirls/core.py lines 98-107: the `make_measurement_operator` function handles `kind in {"fourier", "fourier_rank1"}` by (1) drawing A and B as standard real Gaussians (`rng.standard_normal`), (2) computing FA = `np.fft.fft(a, axis=0)/sqrt(m)` and FB = `np.fft.fft(b, axis=0)/sqrt(m)`, (3) forming rank-one measurements via `np.einsum("mi,mj->mij", fa, np.conj(fb))` — matching y\_j = ⟨(FA)\*\_{j,:} FB\_{j,:}, X⟩\_F. run\_experiments.py line 133 includes "fourier" in `kinds = ["gaussian", "rank1", "fourier"]` for the operator comparison experiment.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The FFT is applied along axis=0 (over m rows) rather than per-row, but the construction of rank-one outer products (FA)\_j ⊗ conj(FB)\_j still matches the paper's formula. The experiment ID "exp7\_fourier\_rank1\_measurements\_r1" is not explicitly named in the code but the Fourier rank-one operator is implemented and used in comparison experiments.

N(0,1) (when condition\_number is None); (5) x = (left \* scales\[None, :\]) @ right.T computes X̃⋆ = U⋆ diag(d⋆) V⋆^T; (6) return x / norm normalizes by Frobenius norm. The fourier\_rank1 mode is used in the operator\_robustness experiment which calls this same generate\_ground\_truth function.

Lines 69–76: u is zeros with rows at support set to i.i.d. standard Gaussian (rng.standard\_normal((sparsity, rank))). Line 77: d = rng.standard\_normal(rank) (i.i.d. standard Gaussian). Line 70: v = rng.standard\_normal((n2, rank)) (i.i.d. standard Gaussian). Line 80: x = (u \* d\[None, :\]) @ v.conj().T (= U⋆ diag(d⋆) V⋆^\*). Lines 81–82: x = x / max(np.linalg.norm(x, 'fro'), ...) (Frobenius normalization). run\_experiments.py calls generate\_ground\_truth for all experiments.

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:1168)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/algorithms.py, the irls\_lrrs function initializes eps=np.inf and delta=np.inf. In the first iteration (lines ~88-104), the code immediately updates delta and eps from the current iterate's row norms and singular values (next\_delta, next\_eps), then computes row\_w = 1/max(rows, delta)^2 and sv\_w = 1/max(s, eps)^2 from those updated values. The resulting weights in the first iteration are therefore derived from the initial least-squares solution (via \_initial\_ls), not from the identity matrix. There is no step that sets W=I or row\_w=ones or sv\_w=ones as an initial weight operator.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The identity initialization in the paper's IRLS (Appendix A.1) would require the weight operator to equal I (i.e., all weights equal 1) at the start of iteration 1. This implementation skips that step and immediately computes data-driven weights from the initial LS estimate.

**B：通过，1/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:1168)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In `simirls/core.py`, the `irls_lrrs` function initializes `eps = np.inf` and `delta = np.inf` (lines 173–174). In the first iteration, `active = min(rank, int(np.sum(s > np.inf + 1e-12))) = 0`, so `ua`, `va`, `sa` are empty. In `_weight_apply` (line 117–130), when `u.size == 0` the low-rank part returns `x` unchanged (line 127). With `delta=np.inf`, `row_w = np.inf**(p-2)` and with `p=0` that equals 0, so the row-weight term contributes nothing. The combined weight operator therefore acts as the identity on the first iteration.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The identity initialization is achieved implicitly via eps=inf/delta=inf rather than an explicit `np.eye` call, but the mathematical effect is identical.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

Judge confidence=`medium`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:1234)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/algorithms.py, `irls_lrrs` initializes with `eps = np.inf` and `delta = np.inf` (lines 82-83), corresponding to ε₀=∞, δ₀=∞ → W=Id. The initial iterate X^{(0)} is computed via `_initial_ls` (line 80), which uses the Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse (`np.linalg.pinv`, line 52): `x = (phi.conj().T @ (gram_inv @ y))`. This is exactly the unweighted (W=Id) minimum-norm least-squares solution that Algorithm 2.1 specifies as the identity-weight initialization.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The code updates eps and delta from their infinity values before computing weights in the first loop iteration (lines 96-97), so the weight matrix in the iterative loop is never explicitly formed as identity. However, the identity-weighted initial point X^{(0)} is computed correctly via \_initial\_ls (Moore-Penrose), and eps=inf/delta=inf are set at initialization matching the paper's ε₀=∞, δ₀=∞ → W=Id condition.

**B：未通过，0/1 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:1234)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In simirls/core.py, irls\_lrrs initializes eps=np.inf and delta=np.inf (lines 173-174), but at the start of each iteration (including iteration 0), both are updated to finite values (eps\_target = singular\[rank\], delta\_target based on row norms) before \_weight\_apply is called (lines 183-186). Specifically, since np.isfinite(inf)=False, eps is set to eps\_target on iteration 0, meaning the first weight application uses a finite eps (the (rank+1)-th singular value of X^(0)), not eps=inf. For W=Id to hold at initialization, eps=inf must be passed to \_weight\_apply (which would make active=0 via line 188 and row\_w=0 via delta=inf), but the code never does this — it always computes a non-trivial weight on the first iteration.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The code sets eps=np.inf initially, suggesting intent to match the paper, but the update order (eps updated before weight applied within each iteration) means the identity initialization is not achieved in practice.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> Entries are N(0, 1/m) rather than strictly N(0,1); the 1/sqrt(m) scaling is standard in compressed sensing (ensures A^T A ≈ I) and the matrix is still "Gaussian with i.i.d. entries" in the sense the paper intends.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> run\_experiments.py lines 56-62: y = op.forward(truth) (i.e. A(X\*)); then sigma = np.linalg.norm(y) / np.sqrt(max(m \* snr, 1e-12)) which equals \|\|A(X\*)\|\|\_2 / (sqrt(m) \* SNR); noise = np.random.default\_rng(...).standard\_normal(m) \* sigma (i.i.d. Gaussian entries with std sigma); y = y + noise (additive). This matches the claim exactly.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The `max(..., 1e-12)` guard is a numerical safety clamp that does not affect behavior for any reasonable SNR value.

### 2306.04961 / midloop / setup-130

**检查内容：** IRLS is initialized with the weight operator W\_X^{(0),ε\_0,δ\_0} = Id (identity).

**类别与权重：** `exp_setup`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 0.889%）。

**论文定位：** Algorithm 2.1, Section 4。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_04961/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_midloop.json:2337)

**如何检查：** 目标实验=exp10\_noise\_robustness；数据集=synthetic Gaussian noisy measurements；方法=IRLS；按scope/描述检查是否满足；满足得全部权重，否则得0。

**完整验证规则：**

```json
{
  "type": "boolean",
  "spec": {
    "experiment_id": "exp10_noise_robustness",
    "method": "IRLS",
    "dataset": "synthetic Gaussian noisy measurements"
  }
}
```

**A：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:1575)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In `simirls/algorithms.py`, the IRLS function `irls_lrrs` sets `eps = np.inf` and `delta = np.inf` initially (lines 82-83), but at the start of the first iteration (lines 96-99), these are immediately updated to data-driven values (`next_delta` and `next_eps`) before any weights are computed. The weights are then computed as `row_w = 1/max(rows, delta)^2` and `sv_w = 1/max(s, eps)^2` (lines 102-104). There is no code path where weights are set to 1 (identity) at initialization; the identity weight operator W=Id is never explicitly implemented. The effective first iteration uses finite eps/delta computed from the initial least-squares estimate, not identity weights.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The paper's Algorithm 2.1 specifies initialization with W\_X^{(0),ε\_0,δ\_0} = Id (all weights = 1). This would require either setting initial weights to 1 explicitly, or using ε\_0=δ\_0=∞ and deferring weight computation. The code does neither — it computes and updates eps/delta at the start of the first iteration, so the first weights applied are based on the initial LS solution's row norms and singular values, not identity.

**B：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_midloop.json:1575)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> In `simirls/core.py` lines 173-174, `eps = np.inf` and `delta = np.inf` are set before the IRLS loop. However, at the very start of iteration 0 (lines 183-186), eps and delta are immediately updated to data-dependent values: `eps = max(eps_target, 1e-12)` where `eps_target = singular[rank]` of the current iterate (since `np.isfinite(np.inf)` is False, the branch sets eps = eps\_target directly). The weight `_weight_apply(x, ua, va, sa, delta, p_sparse)` is then called at line 194 using this data-dependent eps/delta. Thus, the first weight applied is NOT identity — it uses eps and delta derived from X^(0)'s SVD and row norms. There is no code path where W=Id (corresponding to eps=∞, delta=∞) is ever actually applied during a weight computation step.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The code does initialize eps=np.inf and delta=np.inf (suggestive of the paper's ε\_0=δ\_0=∞), but the `np.isfinite` branch causes these to be replaced by data-dependent values before any weight is computed, making the effective first weight non-identity. This deviates from Algorithm 2.1's initialization W^(0) = Id.

In simirls/experiments.py lines 21-26 and 49-52, `noise_robustness` uses `n1=256, n2=40, rank=1, sparsity=40`, exactly matching the rubric dimensions (256×1 left factor with 40 non-zero rows, 40×1 right factor, scalar d).

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

Gaussian entries for U⋆ ∈ R^{256×1}; (3) d = rng.standard\_normal(rank=1) — i.i.d. Gaussian; (4) v = rng.standard\_normal((n2=40, rank=1)) — V⋆ ∈ R^{40×1} i.i.d. Gaussian; (5) x = (u \* d\[None,:\]) @ v.conj().T — computes X̃⋆ = U⋆ diag(d⋆) V⋆^\*; (6) x = x / np.linalg.norm(x, "fro") — normalizes. run\_one\_noise.py line 17 calls with n1=256, n2=40, rank=1, sparsity=40, matching the paper dimensions exactly.

All three algorithms compute ‖X̂ - X⋆‖\_F / ‖X⋆‖\_F when x\_true is provided. In simirls/experiments.py the result column is stored as "final\_relative\_frobenius" (line 90) and the plot y-axis label is "median relative Frobenius error" (line 126). np.linalg.norm on a 2D array defaults to the Frobenius norm.

This is called in run\_experiments.py at line 71 (`err = relative_error(rec, truth)`) within the noise robustness experiment (`run_noise` function, line 113+) which uses dense Gaussian operators and records `relative_error` in results (line 126). The metric matches ∥X̂ - X⋆∥\_F / ∥X⋆∥\_F exactly.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The small epsilon (1e-14) in the denominator is a numerical guard for the zero-truth edge case; it does not change the semantics of the metric.

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> The only relevant run output is results/formal/phase\_transition\_dense\_rank1/metrics.csv (72 rows, m ∈ {160, 200, 240}, 8 trials each). All 72 rows have success=False for every algorithm (IRLS-LRRS, RiemannianIHT, SparsePowerFactorization). No oversampling\_factor or phase-transition threshold can be derived because no algorithm ever crosses a 50% (or any) success rate in the measured range. At m=240 (the highest), median relative Frobenius errors are: IRLS-LRRS≈0.23, RiemannianIHT≈0.68, SparsePowerFactorization≈0.77 — which suggests IRLS has smaller error, but since no success threshold is crossed, no oversampling\_factor comparison is possible. The ranking claim (IRLS achieves lower oversampling\_factor than SPF and RiemAdaIHT) cannot be verified from these outputs.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The experiment was run with an insufficient range of m values (only 3 points, all below the phase transition). The outputs do not record oversampling\_factor as a metric. Final relative Frobenius errors after max iterations: IRLS-LRRS=4.769e-4 (80 iters), SparsePowerFactorization=0.6455 (80 iters), RiemannianIHT=0.4340 (120 iters). IRLS-LRRS is dramatically lower-error than both SPF and RiemAdaIHT, satisfying IRLS \> SPF and IRLS \> RiemAdaIHT under lower\_is\_better. Error traces confirm SPF and RiemAdaIHT show near-constant ratio (~0.998–0.9998 per iteration), consistent with slow linear convergence. IRLS shows much faster reduction overall (0.777→4.8e-4 vs 0.835→0.646 for SPF and 0.779→0.441 for RiemAdaIHT).

The precise characterization of "quadratic convergence within ~10 iterations" for IRLS is harder to confirm from the traces alone (the first 10 iters show error 0.777→0.218, which looks more linear than quadratic), but the required ranking order (IRLS better than both SPF and RiemAdaIHT) is strongly satisfied. The verification type is 'ranking', which is what this verdict addresses.

Final errors: IRLS-LRRS avg=0.749, SPF avg=0.997, RiemAdaIHT avg=0.636. (1) Ranking violation: RiemAdaIHT (0.636) beats IRLS (0.749) — the required order IRLS \> RiemAdaIHT does not hold. (2) Quadratic convergence claim unsupported: IRLS relative\_change per iteration is INCREASING (0.092→0.127, ratios ~1.02–1.09 per step), showing no convergence at all, not quadratic convergence. RiemAdaIHT relative\_change decreases consistently (0.322→0.055, ratios ~0.73–0.84), showing linear convergence as stated in the paper for it, but IRLS does NOT outperform it on the error metric.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The CSV uses method name 'IRLS-LRRS' (not 'IRLS'), but config and context confirm this is the IRLS method in the claim. The metric column 'relative\_change' appears to capture per-iteration step size, not relative Frobenius error to the true solution; 'final\_error' captures the terminal relative Frobenius error. Neither metric supports the claim.

### 2306.04961 / bigloop / res-016

**检查内容：** The median relative Frobenius reconstruction error of IRLS, SPF, and RiemAdaIHT is roughly proportional to the inverse square root of the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR).

**类别与权重：** `exp_results`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 7.143%）。

**论文定位：** Appendix A.5, Figure 7。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_04961/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_bigloop.json:351)

```json
    "scope": "Noise robustness experiment with Gaussian measurements, n1=256, n2=40, s=40, r=1, oversampling factor 3, SNR from 10 to 10^12"
  }
}
```

**A：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_bigloop.json:200)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> Run outputs: results/formal/noise\_robustness/metrics.csv. Median relative Frobenius errors per algorithm across SNR values {10, 1e3, 1e5, 1e7, 1e9}: IRLS-LRRS: \[0.503, 0.227, 0.224, 0.305, 0.223\]; SPF: \[0.870, 0.825, 0.769, 0.817, 0.845\]; RiemAdaIHT: \[0.814, 0.735, 0.677, 0.777, 0.776\]. Log-log slopes (expected -0.5 for 1/sqrt(SNR) scaling): IRLS=-0.029, SPF=-0.001, RiemAdaIHT=-0.001. Errors are essentially flat across all SNR values. All 120 instances recorded success=False (algorithms hit iteration limits without converging), so errors reflect non-convergence, not noise-proportional behavior. The claimed 1/sqrt(SNR) proportionality is completely absent in the run outputs.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The experiment used only 8 instances per SNR level (not the oversampling factor 3 or other parameters exactly matching the paper's Figure 7 description), but even so the flat error curves with slopes near 0 (vs expected -0.5) conclusively refute the claim. The algorithms never converged in any trial, so no meaningful scaling trend could emerge.

**B：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_bigloop.json:200)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> Run outputs found in results/formal/noise\_20260905/metrics.csv (27 rows, SNR ∈ {10, 1000, 1000000}, 3 seeds each). Computed median relative Frobenius errors per method per SNR:
> 
> IRLS-LRRS: SNR=10 → 0.9851, SNR=1000 → 0.9835, SNR=1e6 → 0.9834. Ratios (SNR10→1000, 1000→1e6): 0.9984, 0.9999. Expected ~0.1 for 1/sqrt(SNR) scaling — observed ratio is ~1 (no scaling).
> 
> RiemAdaIHT: SNR=10 → 0.9560, SNR=1000 → 0.9540, SNR=1e6 → 0.9538. Ratios: 0.9979, 0.9998. Again essentially constant — no 1/sqrt(SNR) dependence.
> 
> SPF: SNR=10 → 0.4161, SNR=1000 → 0.0442, SNR=1e6 → 0.0351. Ratio SNR10→1000: 0.106 (consistent with 1/sqrt(SNR)), but ratio SNR1000→1e6: 0.793 (far from 0.1). Inconsistent.
> 
> All three methods fail to show 1/sqrt(SNR) scaling consistently. IRLS and RiemAdaIHT produce near-constant errors (~0.98 and ~0.95) regardless of SNR, indicating they are not converging to the signal. The experiment also only covers SNR up to 1e6, not the 10 to 10^12 range stated in the claim.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The config shows seeds: \[0, 1\] but the CSV contains 3 seeds (0, 1, 2). The SNR range is limited to {10, 1000, 1e6} versus the claimed 10 to 10^12. The near-unity errors for IRLS and RiemAdaIHT suggest algorithmic failure (non-convergence), so the 1/sqrt(SNR) claim cannot be verified from these outputs.

### 2306.04961 / bigloop / res-017

**检查内容：** IRLS is as noise robust as SPF and RiemAdaIHT, achieving reconstruction error of the order of the noise norm.

**类别与权重：** `exp_results`；`primary`；权重 2（本层占比 7.143%）。

**论文定位：** Appendix A.5。[原始量规](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/judge/rubrics/CPU30_2306_04961/CPU30_llm_generated_v2/files/rubrics_llm_bigloop.json:367)


**A：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/Codex原生-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_bigloop.json:211)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> results/formal/noise\_robustness/metrics.csv and metrics.json: At SNR=1e9 (noise level ~3.16e-5), IRLS-LRRS mean relative Frobenius error = 0.282, SparsePowerFactorization = 0.868, RiemannianIHT = 0.801. At SNR=1e1 (noise level ~0.316), IRLS = 0.509, SPF = 0.870, RiemIHT = 0.826. None of the three methods achieve reconstruction error of the order of the noise norm — errors plateau at 0.27–0.87 regardless of SNR spanning 1e1 to 1e9, meaning at high SNR the error is ~4 orders of magnitude larger than the noise level. All 120 instance runs have success=False. While IRLS is relatively better than the other two methods, the qualitative claim that all three achieve O(noise norm) reconstruction error is not supported.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The claim might be intended to compare the three methods' noise robustness relative to each other (IRLS ≥ the other two), but the specific statement "achieving reconstruction error of the order of the noise norm" requires error ∝ noise level, which the data contradicts — errors do not decrease proportionally with noise for any method tested.

**B：未通过，0/2 分。**

Judge confidence=`high`；error=`false`。[该项判词](/Users/zanehuang/work/OptiRepro/reports/cpu30/analysis-2026-09-06/evidence/OptiRepro_cpu_2/agent_reproduction/runs/2306.04961/ReasFlow实验Agent-完整复现-Goal/第003次运行/judge/evaluations/第001次评测/results/evaluation_bigloop.json:211)

**证据（Judge 原文）：**

> results/formal/noise\_20260905/metrics.csv: IRLS-LRRS has relative\_error ≈ 0.983 (mean across seeds) at SNR=10, 0.983 at SNR=1000, and 0.983 at SNR=1,000,000. The error/noise\_norm ratio grows to ~1000 at SNR=1,000,000 (noise\_norm≈0.001 but error≈0.983), showing IRLS does NOT achieve error of the order of the noise norm. RiemAdaIHT behaves similarly (relative\_error ≈ 0.951 across all SNR, ratio ~1000 at high SNR). SPF actually does improve with SNR (mean error drops from 0.557 at SNR=10 to 0.058 at SNR=1000), contradicting the claim that all three are equally noise-robust. IRLS is far worse than SPF at high SNR, not comparable.

**理由（Judge 原文）：**

> The run outputs for exp10\_noise\_robustness (noise\_20260905) directly contradict the claim. IRLS errors remain flat near ~0.983 regardless of noise level, which means it is NOT noise-robust and does NOT achieve reconstruction error of the order of the noise norm. SPF outperforms both IRLS and RiemAdaIHT at higher SNR, so the "equally robust" comparison also fails.
